##### Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: person.h_ira_lim7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$7,000 (contribution limit if under age 50 at the end of 2025)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit}
  - id: person.h_ira_lim8
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$8,000 (contribution limit if age 50 or older at the end of 2025)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit_50}
  - id: person.h_ira_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$7,000 if under age 50 at the end of 2025; $8,000 if age 50 or older at the end of 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_ira_lim8
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_lim7
  - id: person.ira_contrib
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Traditional IRA contributions made, or that will be made by the due date of your 2025 return not counting extensions, for 2025 to your IRA (IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 11)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: true}]}
          then: person.ira_trad
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    note: "A person with no IRA of any kind has made no traditional IRA contributions; the contribution amount is read only for an IRA owner."
  - id: person.sav_roth_if_ira_owner
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Roth IRA contributions for 2025 (zero if you have no IRA)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: true}]}
          then: person.sav_roth
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
  - id: person.h_ira_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Contributions for 2025 to traditional and Roth IRAs together"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.ira_contrib, person.sav_roth_if_ira_owner]}
  - id: person.ira_excess
    printed: "(screen)"
    label: "Excess contributions: contributions to traditional and Roth IRAs for 2025 more than $7,000 ($8,000 if age 50 or older), or more than you were allowed to contribute, not withdrawn by the due date; or excess contributions for an earlier year remaining in the IRA (tax on excess contributions, Form 5329)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_extra_left, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [person.h_ira_total, person.h_ira_limit]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.x_ira_prior_excess, {const: true}]}
    note: "Pub. 590-A: an excess contribution is the amount contributed to traditional IRAs that is more than the smaller of $7,000 ($8,000 if age 50 or older) or taxable compensation; the 6% tax applies to excess amounts remaining at the end of the year. The taxable-compensation part is left to the screening answer (person.ira_extra_left); the dollar limit is also tested from the amounts entered. Earlier-year excess remaining (person.x_ira_prior_excess) is included as it also produces the 6% tax."

  # ---------------- Form 8863 Part III, lines 27 to 31 ----------------
  - id: person.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted Qualified Education Expenses Worksheet, line 3: total adjustments (add lines 2a, 2b, and 2c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.edu_a_this_year, person.edu_a_next_year, person.edu_refunds]}
  - id: person.h_edu_over_refused
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Tax-free assistance and refunds allocable to an academic period are more than that period's expenses (worksheet must be completed separately for each academic period)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_over, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [person.h_edu_adj, {const: 0}]}
    note: "The worksheet is completed separately for each academic period and floored at zero per period; one combined worksheet is exact only when no period's adjustments exceed its expenses, so that case is refused."
  - id: person.h_edu_base
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Student with qualified education expenses, a Form 1098-T (or excepted), and a TIN issued by the due date of the return"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.tin, {const: true}]}
    note: "i8863: both credits require the Form 1098-T (or the stated exceptions) and a student TIN issued by the due date (including extensions). person.tin (issued, not merely applied for) is used, following i8863 'has not been issued'."
  - id: person.h_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lines 23 to 26 lead to lines 27 through 30 (American opportunity credit) for this student, with the institution's EIN provided"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_base, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_ein, {const: true}]}
    note: "Line 23 Yes, line 24 No, line 25 Yes or line 26 Yes each send the student to line 31. The EIN must be provided to claim the American opportunity credit; without it the student was read as going to line 31 (the lifetime learning credit does not require it)."
  - id: person.h_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 31 (lifetime learning credit) is completed for this student"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_edu_base, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_aoc, {const: false}]}
  - id: person.h_edu_ws1_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted Qualified Education Expenses Worksheet, line 1 (American opportunity credit): tuition, required fees and course materials needed for a course of study"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.edu_inst, person.edu_mat]}
  - id: person.h_edu_ws4_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (American opportunity credit). Subtract line 3 from line 1. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_edu_ws1_aoc, take: person.h_edu_adj}
  - id: person.h_edu_cap
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.expense_cap}
  - id: person.h_edu_l27
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses, not more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_edu_ws4_aoc, person.h_edu_cap]}
  - id: person.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_edu_l27
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_aoc, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    refuses: [person.h_edu_over_refused]
    shown_when: person.h_aoc
  - id: person.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.edu_line27, take: {param: aoc.full_band}}
    shown_when: person.h_aoc
  - id: person.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.edu_line28, rate: {param: aoc.second_rate}}
    shown_when: person.h_aoc
  - id: person.h_edu_band
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$2,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: aoc.full_band}
  - id: person.h_edu_l29_plus
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add $2,000 to the amount on line 29"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.edu_line29, person.h_edu_band]}
  - id: person.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}
          then: person.edu_line27
        - guard: {op: test, ne: [person.edu_line28, {const: 0}]}
          then: person.h_edu_l29_plus
    shown_when: person.h_aoc
  - id: person.h_edu_ws4_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (lifetime learning credit): tuition, required fees and materials required to be paid to the institution, less total adjustments; if zero or less, -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.edu_inst, take: person.h_edu_adj}
    note: "For the lifetime learning credit, books, supplies and equipment count only if required to be paid to the institution, so person.edu_mat is left out."
  - id: person.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Include the total of all amounts from all Parts III, line 31, on Part II, line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_llc, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_edu_ws4_llc
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_llc, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    refuses: [person.h_edu_over_refused]
    shown_when: person.h_llc

  # ---------------- Refundable AOC restriction (Form 8863 line 7 box) ----------------
  - id: person.h_age18plus
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Age 18 or older at the end of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_18}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_18}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.born_jan1, {const: true}]}
    note: "A person is considered to reach an age on the day before the birthday, so a person born January 1 of the cut-off year counts as that age at the end of 2025."
  - id: person.h_age19plus
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Over age 18 at the end of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_19}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_19}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.born_jan1, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.h_age24plus
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Age 24 or older at the end of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, lt: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_24}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.birth_year, {param: age.cut_24}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [person.born_jan1, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.edu_kid
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can't take the refundable American opportunity credit: (a) under age 18 at the end of 2025, or age 18 with earned income less than one-half of support, or over 18 and under 24, a full-time student, with earned income less than one-half of support; and at least one parent alive at the end of 2025; and not filing a joint return"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.h_age18plus, {const: false}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [person.h_age18plus, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.h_age19plus, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_half_support, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [person.h_age19plus, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.h_age24plus, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_fulltime, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_half_support, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.edu_parent, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}

  # ---------------- Schedule C counts ----------------
  - id: person.ho_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of this person's businesses claiming a deduction for business use of the home"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: sc
      field: sc.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [sc.ho_uses, {const: true}]}
    note: "Read as the count of Schedules C using the home (i1040sc: the 300 square foot maximum applies to all businesses conducted in the home)."
  - id: person.sc_credit_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of this person's businesses claiming a general business credit (Form 3800)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: sc
      field: sc.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [sc.business_credit, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.sc_fuel_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of this person's businesses claiming a credit for federal tax on fuels (Form 4136)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: sc
      field: sc.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [sc.fuel_credit, {const: true}]}

  # ---------------- Schedule 1, line 16 ----------------
  - id: person.h_sep_ded
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Actual SEP contribution, not to exceed your maximum deductible contribution (Deduction Worksheet for Self-Employed, step 21)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.sep_contrib, person.sep_s21]}
  - id: person.h_simple_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "SIMPLE IRA salary reduction and employer contributions made for yourself"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.simple_deferral, person.simple_employer]}
  - id: person.s16
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Self-employed SEP, SIMPLE, and qualified plans (this person's share of Schedule 1, line 16)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.sep_shown, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_sep_ded
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.sep_shown, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}
          then: person.h_simple_total
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.sep_shown, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, ne: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}
          then: person.zero
    refuses: [person.plan_screen]
    note: "The SEP deduction reads step 21 only where the SEP worksheet is completed (person.sep_shown, assumed true exactly for a SEP). For a SIMPLE IRA, Pub. 560 has sole proprietors deduct contributions for themselves on line 16; contributions above the maximum deductible amount are screened out by person.plan_screen, so the contributions made are the deduction."

  # ---------------- AMT ----------------
  - id: person.amt_dep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Depreciation on property placed in service before 2025 (Form 4562, line 17) that may need refiguring for the AMT (Form 6251, line 2l)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: sc, field: sc.f17}
    note: "The meaning of this interface is not determined by the pages. Read as the MACRS on earlier-year property, the only depreciation that can carry an AMT adjustment under i6251 (2025 property eligible for the special allowance and placed in service after 2015 has none); the pages do not let it be split further by method or by election out of the allowance."

  # ---------------- Saver's credit (Form 8880) ----------------
  - id: person.h_sav_trad
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Traditional IRA contributions for 2025 (not rollovers)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.sav_trad, {const: true}]}
          then: person.ira_contrib
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.sav_trad, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
  - id: person.sav_line1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8880, line 1: Traditional and Roth IRA contributions, and ABLE account contributions by the designated beneficiary for 2025. Do not include rollover contributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_sav_trad, person.sav_roth_if_ira_owner]}
    refuses: [person.sav_able]
  - id: person.h_sav_w2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Elective deferrals shown in box 12 of this person's Forms W-2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.sav_deferral}
  - id: person.h_sav_simple
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Elective deferrals to your own SIMPLE IRA as a self-employed person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}
          then: person.simple_deferral
        - guard: {op: test, ne: [person.plan, {const: "simple"}]}
          then: person.zero
  - id: person.sav_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8880, line 2: Elective deferrals to a 401(k) or other qualified employer plan, voluntary employee contributions, and 501(c)(18)(D) plan contributions for 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_sav_w2, person.sav_voluntary, person.h_sav_simple]}

  # ---------------- IRA Deduction Worksheet (this person's column) ----------------
  - id: person.h_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing jointly or qualifying surviving spouse"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "qss"}]}
  - id: person.h_jc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing jointly or qualifying surviving spouse, in the column of a person covered by a retirement plan"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_joint, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_work_plan, {const: true}]}
  - id: person.h_mfs_apart
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing separately and you lived apart from your spouse for all of 2025 (line 2 amount $89,000)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [ssb.lived_apart, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [ira.anyone_covered, {const: true}]}
    note: "No supplied parameter gives $89,000 for a separate return (ira.line2 holds $10,000 for mfs), so this case is refused rather than computed."
  - id: person.h_l2_nc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$246,000 (married filing jointly, person who wasn't covered by a plan)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.line2_not_covered}
  - id: person.h_l2_std
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$89,000 single or head of household; $146,000 qualifying surviving spouse or married filing jointly; $10,000 married filing separately and lived with spouse"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.line2}
  - id: person.h_ira_l2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 2 (this person's column)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_work_plan, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_l2_nc
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_work_plan, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_l2_std
  - id: person.h_ira_l6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 6: subtract line 5 from line 2 (zero when line 5 is not less than line 2: none of your IRA contributions are deductible)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_ira_l2, take: ira.line5}
  - id: person.h_full_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$20,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_joint}
  - id: person.h_full_narrow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_narrow}
  - id: person.h_ira_thr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$20,000 if married filing jointly or qualifying surviving spouse ($10,000 in the column of a person not covered); $10,000 otherwise"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_jc, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_full_joint
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_jc, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_full_narrow
  - id: person.h_ira_p70
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 70% (0.70)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_ira_l6, rate: {param: ira.rate_70}}
  - id: person.h_ira_p80
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 80% (0.80)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_ira_l6, rate: {param: ira.rate_80}}
  - id: person.h_ira_p35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 35% (0.35)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_ira_l6, rate: {param: ira.rate_35}}
  - id: person.h_ira_p40
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 40% (0.40)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_ira_l6, rate: {param: ira.rate_40}}
  - id: person.h_ira_p
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 7: line 6 multiplied by the applicable percentage"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_jc, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p40
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_jc, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p35
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_jc, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p80
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_jc, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p70
  - id: person.h_ira_p_up
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If the result isn't a multiple of $10, increase it to the next multiple of $10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: round, of: person.h_ira_p, unit: 1000, rule: up}
  - id: person.h_ira_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.min}
  - id: person.h_ira_l7r
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If the result is $200 or more, enter the result. But if it is less than $200, enter $200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [person.h_ira_p_up, person.h_ira_min]}
  - id: person.h_ira_l7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 7 (this person's column)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [ira.anyone_covered, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_limit
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [ira.anyone_covered, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, ge: [person.h_ira_l6, person.h_ira_thr]}
          then: person.h_ira_limit
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [ira.anyone_covered, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_l6, {const: 0}]}
          then: person.zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [ira.anyone_covered, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, gt: [person.h_ira_l6, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, lt: [person.h_ira_l6, person.h_ira_thr]}
          then: person.h_ira_l7r
    note: "The line 6 STOP (none deductible) is read per column: a zero line 6 gives a zero line 7 for that column."
  - id: person.h_ira_l12a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 7 or line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_l7, ira.line10]}
  - id: person.ira_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 12: the smallest of line 7, 10, or 11. This is the most you can deduct"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_l12a, person.ira_contrib]}
    refuses: [person.ira_excess, person.ira_recharacterized, person.h_mfs_apart, ira.deduct_less]
  - id: person.h_ira_n1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of IRA Deduction Worksheet line 10 or line 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [ira.line10, person.ira_contrib]}
  - id: person.ira_nondeductible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606, line 1: subtract line 12 of the IRA Deduction Worksheet from the smaller of line 10 or line 11 of the worksheet"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_ira_n1, take: person.ira_deduction}
  - id: person.ira_8606_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606, line 2: Enter your total basis in traditional IRAs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.ira_basis}
    refuses: [person.ira_basis_adjusted]
  - id: person.h_8606_line3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606, line 3: Add lines 1 and 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.ira_nondeductible, person.ira_8606_line2]}
  - id: person.ira_dist_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You took distributions from a traditional IRA (or made a Roth IRA conversion) in 2025 and you made nondeductible contributions to a traditional IRA in 2025 or an earlier year (Form 8606, lines 4 through 13 required)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_owner, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [person.h_8606_line3, {const: 0}]}
  - id: person.ira_files_8606
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "File Form 8606: nondeductible contributions to a traditional IRA for 2025, or distributions from traditional IRAs with a basis"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_nondeductible, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist_gate, {const: true}]}
    note: "Covers the Who Must File conditions expressible from the interface; a conversion with no basis, Roth IRA distributions and divorce transfers are not separately asked."
  - id: person.ira_ssb
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Contributions to a traditional IRA for 2025 (Social Security Benefits Worksheet exception)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.ira_contrib}
    note: "Meaning not determined by the pages. Read as this person's traditional IRA contributions for 2025, which with plan coverage trigger the Pub. 590-A exception to the Social Security Benefits Worksheet."
  - id: person.ira_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA Deduction Worksheet, line 9 (this person): net earnings from self-employment minus deductions on Schedule 1, lines 15 and 16. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.sep_s3, take: person.s16}
    note: "Net earnings from self-employment taken as step 3 of the Deduction Worksheet for Self-Employed (Schedule C net profit less the Schedule 1, line 15 deduction), computed whether or not displayed."

  # ---------------- Form 5329 exception dollar limits (per person) ----------------
  - id: person.h_x09
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Distributions covered by exception 09 (first home)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.exc_amount
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.exception, {const: "e09"}]}
  - id: person.h_x19
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Distributions covered by exception 19 (birth or adoption)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.exc_amount
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.exception, {const: "e19"}]}
  - id: person.h_x22
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Distributions covered by exception 22 (domestic abuse)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.exc_amount
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.exception, {const: "e22"}]}
  - id: person.h_x23
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Distributions covered by exception 23 (emergency personal expense)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.exc_amount
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.exception, {const: "e23"}]}
  - id: person.h_cfh
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5329.cap_first_home}
  - id: person.h_cap09
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,000 less all prior qualified first-time homebuyer distributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_cfh, take: person.x_prior09}
  - id: person.h_cb
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$5,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5329.cap_birth}
  - id: person.h_cap19
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$5,000 for each birth or adoption"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_count, amount: person.h_cb, count: person.x_births}
  - id: person.h_cap22
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,300"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5329.cap_abuse}
  - id: person.h_cap23
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$1,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: f5329.cap_emergency}
  - id: person.h_m09
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "First home exception, limited"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_x09, person.h_cap09]}
  - id: person.h_m19
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Birth or adoption exception, limited"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_x19, person.h_cap19]}
  - id: person.h_m22
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Domestic abuse exception, limited"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_x22, person.h_cap22]}
  - id: person.h_m23
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Emergency personal expense exception, limited"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_x23, person.h_cap23]}
  - id: person.x_limited_sum
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Amounts excluded under the exceptions with per-taxpayer dollar limits (first home, birth or adoption, domestic abuse, emergency personal expense), each within its limit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.h_m09, person.h_m19, person.h_m22, person.h_m23]}
    note: "Meaning not determined by the pages. Read as the per-person total of the dollar-limited exceptions (Pub. 590-B and i5329: $10,000 less prior first-home distributions; $5,000 per birth or adoption; $10,300; $1,000), each limited."
  - id: person.x_limit_binds
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "A per-taxpayer dollar limit on an exception is reached by the amounts claimed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.h_x09, person.h_cap09]}
        - {op: test, gt: [person.h_x19, person.h_cap19]}
        - {op: test, gt: [person.h_x22, person.h_cap22]}
        - {op: test, gt: [person.h_x23, person.h_cap23]}
    note: "Read as: the amounts entered for some limited exception exceed its per-person limit, so the limit must be allocated among distributions."
```

Second transcription's note: Pub. 590-A: an excess contribution is the amount contributed to traditional IRAs that is more than the smaller of $7,000 ($8,000 if age 50 or older) or taxable compensation; the 6% tax applies to excess amounts remaining at the end of the year. The taxable-compensation part is left to the screening answer (person.ira_extra_left); the dollar limit is also tested from the amounts entered. Earlier-year excess remaining (person.x_ira_prior_excess) is included as it also produces the 6% tax.
